### 9.101 Values read before a suspension (M6.0b2)

- **Lowering** (D-145, `pts/lower/yield_expressions.go`): `isSettled` keeps every property and element read, and settles a name only through `isUnchangedWhileSuspended` (constants, functions and the like; the function's own variables unless `assignedOutside`; outer variables unless `assignedAnywhere`, which `collectOutsideAssignments` now also fills); unary `+`/`-`/`~` and templates need primitive operands (`isPrimitive`); `hoistYields` keeps a template's non-primitive substitution as its string (`keepString`) and, for a compound assignment whose right side yields (`readsTargetFirst`), the target's value (`keepCurrent`, into `l.keptCurrent`, which `compoundAssignment` reads, and `readKeptAndStore` instead of calling a getter again); `evaluationOrder` puts a called name before the arguments, and a callee that is an access after its object and key. H08a (D-187): postfix `++`/`--`, `in` and binary operators that would convert a non-primitive operand aren't settled; `isSettledCallee` settles methods (`o["m"]` too), functions, the libraries' members and members of unions of objects, and `keep` keeps any other callee's value (`keepCallee`, `DynamicMethodOf`, for a member found at run time, which `dynamicMethodCall` then calls with `DynamicCallFound`); `hoistLogicalAssignment` reads a logical assignment's place once (`placeValue`), tests it (`truthOf`, `storesNullish`) and stores the right side, its suspensions hoisted into the branch, as a plain assignment; `evaluationOrder` lists an object literal's shorthand values and computed keys, and `keep` reads a shorthand's through `shorthandValue`, which reads what was kept. H08b (D-188): `keep` keeps a spread as what it spreads then. `keepSpread` copies the array, tuple, typed array, map or set it iterates, or for `a.keys()`, `a.values()` and `a.entries()` their receiver (`iterationReceiver`), in its own IR type, and drains a union, dynamic value or object into an `any[]` (`spreadSnapshot`), so `argumentValues`, `spreadParts` and `dynamicCallWith` lower the spread from the kept local as before; `keepObjectSpread` lists the `SpreadAssignment` in `evaluationOrder`, copies a hash-map object (`NewMap{From}`) and reads another object's properties into an object of a values-only shape (`propertiesCopy`, `valuesShape`, `OrderOf` the source), whose names and source type `copiedNames` hands `spreadObjectLiteral` and `spreadIntoRecord`; `isSettled` settles a spread only of a settled string or array literal.
- **Spreads at their place** (D-189, H08c): `spread` (`pts/cgen/arrays.go`) writes an `ArraySpread` as statements that evaluate and append each operand in turn (`concat` keeps `a.concat`'s order; both use `appended`); `copyChangedSpreads`, `spreadCopy` and `isNewArray` (`cgen.go`) copy a console method's or dynamic call's spread where it is evaluated when a later argument may write; `dynamicSpread` (`pts/lower/dynamic.go`) hands a non-array spread through `any` to `ir.ValueSpreadItems` (`pts_value_spread_items`: arrays, tuples and typed arrays as they are, anything else's items in a new array); `pts_value_to_array` allocates its result at its final length (`dynamic_methods.c`).
- **C** (`pts/cgen/order.go`): `effectsOf` handles every IR expression (constants, `Resumed`/`ResumedBy`; anything else reads, writes and throws); `builtinEffects` classifies builtins (`computedBuiltins`, the String/Number/Math members, `throwingBuiltins`, the order-dependent `Math.random` and `performance.now`, console); `ToString` of a dynamic value counts as a call.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/suspension_snapshots.pts` (the review's probe 6; compound assignments to names, fields and elements; an element, a name and a callee read before an await; a template's toString before a yield; unchanged locals; against Node). H08a: `run/compound_assign_suspension_reads_target_first`, `logical_assign_suspensions`, `logical_assign_suspends_only_when_storing`, `logical_assign_suspension_unset_fields`, `logical_assign_suspension_values`, `call_suspension_reads_callee_first`, `call_suspension_narrowed_callees`, `suspension_reference_operands_evaluate_once`, `suspension_operands_evaluate_first`, `suspension_literal_operands_evaluate_first`, `errors/union_callee_suspension`; `pts/runtime/tests/dynamic_test.c` (`pts_dynamic_method_of`, `pts_dynamic_call_found`); H08b: `run/suspension_spreads_evaluate_first`, `suspension_spread_arguments_evaluate_first`, `suspension_spread_iterables_evaluate_first`, `suspension_object_spreads_evaluate_first`, `suspension_spreads_in_other_forms`, `suspension_spread_copies`, `errors/object_spread_suspension`; H08c: `run/spread_elements_read_before_later_arguments` (unskipped), `spreads_read_before_later_operands_at_every_site`, `any_call_spreads_iterables`, `any_and_console_spreads_read_at_their_place`; `pts/runtime/tests/dynamic_test.c` (`pts_value_spread_items`).

### 9.102 The iteration protocol's order (M6.0b3)

- **Runtime** (D-146, `generator.c`): `resume_object_iterator` reads `done`, then `value` only for a result that isn't done; a done result goes in `object_iterator.finished` with the value `PTS_VALUE_ABSENT`, which `pts_iterator_value` turns into a read of the result's `value`.
- **Lowering** (`exceptions.go`, `generators.go`, `async_iteration.go`): `withFinally` passes its `finallyContext` to the finally code; `iteratorClosing` (used by `iteratorForOf` and `asyncForOf`) closes the iterator inside a try that drops its error when the completion is a throw, and plainly otherwise.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/iterator_close_order.pts` (the review's probe 13; break, return and non-object results; yield*'s done then value; for await; against Node).

### 9.103 Type arguments in conversion messages (M6.0b4a)

- **Lowering** (D-147, `generic_classes.go`): `classInstanceOf` sets `ir.Class.Display` from `instanceDisplay` (the checker's spelling of each type argument).
- **C** (`classes.go`, `checks.go`, `cgen.go`): a class descriptor gets `.display_name`; instance checks and checked conversions name the class with `typeName` (its `Display` when set).
- **Runtime** (`pts.h`, `dynamic.c`): `pts_type.display_name`; `pts_value_description` prefers it.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/generic_conversion_messages.pts`.

### 9.104 Typed calls of erased copies find instances (M6.0b4b)

- **Lowering** (D-148, `instances.go`): `instanceValueCall` (from `valueCall`) and `dispatchedInstanceCall` (from `dispatchedGenericCall`) record a `demand` and lower the call to `ir.InstanceCall` over locals (the callee or receiver, then the typed arguments), whose `Otherwise` is the erased call. `addErasedValue` records erased values (from `genericValue`, `genericMethodValue` for statics, and `functionExpression` for generic consts). `specialize`, the last step of the lowering loop, pairs demands with values (`specializeValue`) and with classes' generic methods (`specializeMethod`), filling `ir.Func.Instances` and `ir.Class.Instances`; an instance's code that its type arguments make dead lowers as a trap (D-195; `narrowsAway`, which skipped such instances, is gone).
- **C** (`classes.go`, `closures.go`, `checks.go`): `instanceTable` writes a descriptor's `pts_instance` table with an entry function per instance (the call's exact C signature, passing the arguments the instance takes and casting a subclass result); `instanceCall` writes the lookup and the two calls; `collectInstanceSignatures` names the signatures' checks.
- **Runtime** (`pts.h`, `object.c`): `pts_instance`, `pts_type.instance_count` and `.instances`, `pts_instance_find` (a closure's own table, or the object's class and its ancestors, stopping at an entry without a function).
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/generic_factories.pts` (against Node), `pts/tests/run/generic_factories_fallbacks.pts`, `object_test.c`'s `instances_are_found_by_name_and_signature`; the corpus program `oop/result_option.pts` is no longer skipped.

### 9.106 Frozen objects (M6.0c2)

- **Runtime** (D-157, `object.c`): `pts_value_freeze` points an object, tuple, array, hash-map object or RegExp (an object only if it has fields) at its descriptor's frozen twin (`frozen_twin`: a copy of `descriptor_size` bytes, method table included, with `frozen` set and `parent` the original; one per descriptor, never freed), marks the other kinds in D-105's table, and throws for a typed array with elements; `pts_value_is_frozen` tests either. `pts_unfrozen` (`pts.h`) sees through a twin wherever the runtime compares descriptors (RegExps, match arrays, copies' descriptors, conversions, spread order). The errors: `pts_throw_read_only`, `pts_throw_not_extensible`, `pts_throw_undeletable`, `pts_throw_frozen_field` (`object.c`), `pts_throw_frozen_element` (`array.c`), `pts_throw_frozen_key` (`map.c`). Stores through sites: `pts_field_store_address`, `pts_field_set_slow`, `pts_property_find_slow` (setter lookups), `pts_optional_set_slow` throw before caching, so no store site caches a twin. Arrays: every mutator in `array.c` throws at the first write JS's algorithm makes (`frozen_pop`, `frozen_shift`, `frozen_unshift`, `frozen_splice`; `sort` after the comparator's calls). Hash-map objects: `pts_record_set`, `pts_record_delete` (`map.c`), which `PTS_RECORD_PUT` and `MapSet`/`MapDelete` of records call; a Map's and Set's `pts_map_set` test nothing. `any`: `object_set` and `pts_dynamic_set` (`dynamic.c`). RegExps: `pts_regexp_set_last_index` (`regexp_object.c`) for every write of `lastIndex`, and `search` writes it only when JS does (`regexp_string.c`).
- **C** (`classes.go`, `checks.go`, `order.go`): `collectChecks` sets `emitter.freezes` for a program with `ir.ValueFreeze`; there, `fieldStore` evaluates the value, then stores through `PTS_WRITABLE` (a test of the descriptor's `frozen` flag), except to `#private` fields; stores through interfaces use `pts_field_store_address` (`fieldPlace`) in every program. A class descriptor with a method table has `.descriptor_size`. Field stores, array mutators and records' puts and deletes may throw (`effectsOf`, `arrayEffects`, `mapEffects`).
- **Lowering** (`generics.go`, `lower.go`): `readonlyInstance` makes `Readonly<X>` of a class's instance type, `this` or a library type the type X in `instantiate`, so `Object.freeze(this)`, `Readonly<Point>` parameters and frozen RegExps, Maps and Dates lower as their own types; `memberSymbol` and `libMemberName` take X's members.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/object_freeze_writes.pts` and `object_freeze_arrays.pts` (against Node: classes, subclasses, literals, optional properties, interfaces, `any`, `Object.assign`, setters, `#private`, errors, `Readonly<X>`, every array mutator's first failing write, hash-map objects, tuples, RegExps, typed arrays, Maps, Sets, Dates, copies); `object_test.c`'s `frozen_objects_point_to_a_shared_twin` and `stores_to_frozen_objects_throw`.

### 9.107 Asserted union literals (M6.0c3)

- **Runtime** (D-158, `object.c`): `missing_member` replaces the panics of `pts_field_address_slow`, `pts_property_find_slow`, `pts_method_find_slow` and `pts_method_find_adapted_slow` for a member the object lacks; `pts_field_store_address_slow`, `pts_field_set_slow` and `pts_property_find_slow` (setters) throw `pts_throw_not_extensible` for a write to one. A property of another representation was already a TypeError (`wrong_kind`): every read through a union of object types converts the union's value, which makes the program carry declared types (D-072).
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/asserted_union_literals.pts`.

### 9.108 fetch (M6.3a)

- **Runtime** (D-160, `fetch.c`): `pts_http_fetch` (`pts.h`) writes the request, resolves (`on_resolved`), connects to each address in turn (`try_next`, `on_connected`, `on_closed_retry`), reads (`on_alloc`, `on_read`) and parses as it goes (`advance`, `parse_head`, `respond`); `finish` closes the connection and settles the promise; `fail_with` and `fail_system` make the cause. `http_internal.h` shares `pts_http_dechunk`, `pts_http_lists` and `pts_http_equals_ignoring_case` with the server (`http.c`).
- **Library** (`pts/lib/http.pts`): `fetch` (URL parsing in `parseUrl`, `resolveUrl` for redirects, `BAD_PORTS`, `OWN_HEADERS`, `fetchFailed`); `Request` from a `Request`, with `redirect` and a nullable body (`bodyOf`); `Response`'s `url` and `redirected`, and its third constructor parameter for what fetch received.
- **Globals** (`pts/lib/lib.go`, `prelude.d.ts`): `GlobalModules` maps `fetch`, `Request`, `Response` and `Headers` to pts:http; `globalModuleBindings` (`library.go`) binds a global to the export symbol of the module's declaration; `globalModulesUsed` (`frontend/load.go`) adds a module once, however many of its globals a program uses.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/fetch_local.pts` (a `pts:http` server in the same program), `run/fetch_globals.pts` (against Node), `TestFetchFromGoServer` with `http/fetch_client.pts` (chunked, read-until-close, 1xx, malformed and truncated responses, fields of one name, the request as the server sees it).

### 9.109 Aborting fetch (M6.3b1)

- **Library** (D-162, `pts/lib/internal/abort.pts`): `DOMException` (codes in `DOM_EXCEPTION_CODES`), `Event` (its static block hands `beginDispatch`, `endDispatch`, `stopped` and `markTrusted` to `EventTarget`), `EventTarget` (`Registration`s), `AbortSignal` (built only with the module's key, D-184; `signalAbort` aborts and fires the trusted `abort` event; `onabort` adds its listener when first set) and `AbortController`. `pts/lib/http.pts`: `RequestInit.signal`, `Request`'s following signal (`signalOf`, the lazy `signal` getter), `exchange` (one request, its abort listener added while it is in flight), and fetch's checks of the signal.
- **Runtime** (`fetch.c`): `pts_http_fetch` returns an id and calls `done` (`call_back`) instead of settling a promise; ids are `request_slot`s (`remember`, `forget`, `client_of`) in a table under a mutex; `pts_http_fetch_abort` runs `abort_request` on the request's loop (by `pts_post_call` from elsewhere), which cancels the resolver and `finish`es; `resolving` and `retrying` say which callback frees an ended client (`on_resolved`, `on_closed_retry`); `start` runs on the loop (posted by a pool thread); the libuv callbacks run the microtasks, `finish` doesn't. `parse_head` flags a Content-Length over the limit (`too_large`).
- **Front end and lowering:** `GlobalModules` maps the five globals to `pts:internal/abort`; `evaluationOrder` (`frontend/load.go`) visits a global module once even when another one imports it (pts:http imports pts:internal/abort; it ran twice and crashed cgen). Shorthand properties apply tsc's narrowing (`shorthandValue`, `readSymbol` in `unions.go`): `{ callback }` after `callback === null` returns was PTS1001.
- **Tests:** `run/abort_signals.pts` (against Node), `run/fetch_abort.pts` (aborts before sending, while resolving, while the server holds the request, from another loop, of a request a pool thread started; timeouts, reasons; fetch from `parallel` and `.par`; under 1 and 4 threads), `run/fetch_many.pts` (3,000 requests and 1,000 aborted ones leave the open files as they were and grow the process by less than 40 MB), `run/fetch_unhandled.pts` and `run/fetch_unhandled_abort.pts` (exit code 1, against Node), `TestFetchUnderSanitizers` (the first two built with AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer), `TestFetchFromGoServer`'s new cases (a reset mid-body, a declared length over 64 MB and its HEAD, a 20 MB chunked body, fetches nobody awaits, one on another loop).

### 9.110 The server's lifecycle (M6.3b2)

- **Runtime** (D-163, `http.c`): a connection reads on while the program has a request (`dispatch` no longer stops it; `on_read` stops past `MAX_HEAD` buffered bytes) and, on the client's end or an error, `disconnected` closes it and calls the server's `on_disconnect` with the `Request` that `on_request` returned, which `connection_roots` (uncollectable, one per connection) keeps while the request is with the program. `pts_http_respond` writes head and body with one copy (`send_parts`), stops reading after a final response, and sets `draining` when `queued` passes `MAX_QUEUED`; `on_written` resumes reading and `process` below half of it. `process` isn't re-entered from an answer given inside its own `dispatch` (`processing`, replacing `dispatching`). `pts_http_stop(id, close_active)`: `stop_listening` calls `disconnected` for the connections with requests in flight when `close_active`.
- **Library** (`pts/lib/http.pts`): `receive` makes the `Request` and returns it to the runtime (as `unknown`, a `pts_value` that the C side can name exactly), `answer` answers it; `disconnect` (Request's static block) marks the request gone and aborts its signal, which the lazy `signal` getter creates aborted if the client has already gone; `Server.stop(closeActiveConnections)`.
- **Tests:** `TestHTTPServerLifecycle` with `http/lifecycle.pts`, under `PTS_THREADS` 1 and 4 and once built with AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer: a client leaving a held request (its signal aborts, its descriptor closes), one leaving a handler that ignores the signal (closed before the answer, which finds the signal aborted), 10,000 pipelined requests, 64 unread 1 MB responses (the server grows by less than 24 MB; 62 MB without the bound), `stop()` from another loop with a request in flight, an idle connection and a new one, `stop(true)` from a pool thread. `run/fetch_many.pts` now also checks that the server frees the connections of aborted requests it never answers.

### 9.111 Bytes in pts:fs and the file hasher (M6.4a)

- **Runtime** (D-165, `fs.c`): a read job's `as_bytes` makes its result a new Uint8Array (`finish`); `bytes_write_job` copies a typed array's bytes with `pts_copy_shared`; the intrinsics `pts_fs_read_file_bytes`, `pts_fs_write_file_bytes` and their `_async` forms.
- **Library:** `Buffer` and `readsText` (`pts/lib/internal/fs.pts`); `readFileSync`, `writeFileSync`, `appendFileSync` and `writeSync` (`fs.pts`), `readFile`, `writeFile`, `appendFile` and `write` (`fs/promises.pts`), and the default objects' methods.
- **Lowering:** `typedArrayClass` sees through an intersection one member covers (`coveringMember`); `unsupportedToString` (`lower.go`) reports a string conversion of a value whose type's `toString` is tagged `@notSupportedYet`; `isNumberLike` takes a union of number literals as a number (`new Uint8Array(rest < 56 ? 64 : 128)`, `new Array(…)`, `s[…]`; it was PTS1001).
- **Example:** `examples/hasher/main.pts`: SHA-256 over `u32` (wrapping arithmetic, logical `>>`), `int` loop counters, files hashed by `files.par.map(hash)` with `readFileSync` on pool threads, output in sha256sum's format. 200 MB in 8 files: 5.8 s on one thread (Node runs the same code in 4.6 s), 2.1 s on four; array element writes are calls into the runtime and reads check bounds with doubles, which is most of the time (M8).
- **Tests:** `run/fs_bytes.pts` (against Node), `errors/fs_buffer_text.pts` (it replaces `errors/fs_buffers_not_yet.pts`, whose reads now compile), `errors/guide_not_yet.pts`'s Buffer line, `TestExampleHasher` (`examples_test.go`: SHA-256's padding cases, 3 MB of random bytes and a nested directory against Go's `crypto/sha256`, under 1 and 4 threads; a missing file).

### 9.112 The mandelbrot PNG renderer (M6.4b)

- **Example** (D-164, `examples/mandelbrot/main.pts`): `range(0, HEIGHT).par.forEach` paints each row into the PNG's scanlines (one shared `Uint8Array`; rows are disjoint), with the smooth escape count for colour; `encodePng` writes the signature, IHDR, one IDAT holding a zlib stream of stored deflate blocks (at most 65,535 bytes each, `LEN` and `NLEN` little-endian, Adler-32 at the end) and IEND, each chunk with its CRC-32 (`crcTable`, `crc32`, `adler32`, `ByteWriter`), and `writeFileSync` writes the bytes (D-165). 1920×1280 at 1,000 iterations renders in 2.07 s on one thread and 0.31 s on eight (6.8×); the files are identical whatever the thread count.
- **Tests:** `TestExampleMandelbrot` (`examples_test.go`): 240×160 under 1 and 4 threads, the two files equal, decoded by Go's `image/png` (which checks the CRCs, the zlib stream and Adler-32), a point in the main cardioid black, a corner not, more than 50 colours.

### 9.113 The API server, the log analyzer and linear string scans (M6.4c)

- **Examples** (D-166): `examples/api-server/main.pts` (a JSON API on every loop: items in a `Mutex<Map<number, Item>>`, `AtomicInt` counters, `range(…).par.count` in a handler, a wait cut short by `request.signal`, errors thrown as `HttpError` and answered by `serve`'s `error` option, `POST /shutdown` calling `server.stop()`); `examples/log-analyzer/main.pts` (files read by `.par.map`, cut into pieces of about 1M characters at line ends, a RegExp per line, a `Summary` of Maps per piece by `.par.map`, merged and printed); `examples/README.md`.
- **Runtime:** `PTS_STRING_WELL_FORMED` and `pts_string_known_well_formed` (`pts.h`), set by `pts_string_measure`, concatenation (`joined_flags`), `pts_string_repeat` and `pts_string_from_utf8_lossy`; cgen's `staticString` writes it for literals that are valid UTF-8. `pts_regex` holds `code` (with `PCRE2_MATCH_INVALID_UTF`) and `valid_code` (without), and `pts_regex_exec` matches a known well-formed subject with `valid_code` and `PCRE2_NO_UTF_CHECK`. `pts_string_split` uses `split_bytes` unless the separator can match half a surrogate pair. `pts_string_from_ascii` makes slices of ASCII strings (`bytes_between`, `pts_regex_slice`) without measuring them; the lossy decoder measures as it validates; both scans skip 8 ASCII bytes at a time.
- **Tests:** `run/long_string_scans.pts` (against Node: global `replace`, `matchAll` and `split` by RegExp and by string over 1.2-million-character strings that are ASCII, Latin, astral, built by `join` and `repeat`, or sliced, each of which timed out before; separators and subjects with lone surrogates); `strings_without_lone_surrogates_are_known_well_formed` in `string_test.c`; `TestStringConstantsCarryLengthAndFlags` (cgen); `TestExampleAPIServer` and `TestExampleLogAnalyzer` (`examples_test.go`).

### 9.114 The benchmark audit (M6.5a)

- **Benchmarks** (D-167): `pts/bench/spectral_norm` (`main.pts` for ParallelScript, Node and Bun; `main.go` in float64) and `pts/bench/startup` (one line printed; `label`: process start only). Twins changed to do `main.pts`'s work: `sum_reduce/main.ts` (three passes), `mandelbrot/main.ts` (a counting `reduce`) and `main.go` (two passes), `word_count/main.go` and `fan_out/main.go` (the total in a pass of its own), `file_io/main.go` (`Readdirnames`, unsorted; strings made from the bytes read), `word_freq/main.go`, and the `json` and `string_building` checksums (float64 with `math.Mod`).

### 9.115 `pts lsp` (M7.1)

- **Server** (D-168, `pts/lsp`): `ServerOptions` (upstream's `lsp.ServerOptions` with the library's file system wrapped around the given one, automatic type acquisition off, npm refused) and `Run`; `cmd/pts`'s `lsp` command (`--stdio`, the only transport, may be given). It sets two upstream hooks once: `project.ProgramCommandLine` (`programCommandLine`: the project's roots plus `lib.PreludePath`, with `frontend.CompilerOptions()`) and `ls.ExtraDiagnostics` (`ptsDiagnostics`: `check.Program` over `frontend.Of(program, file)`, the modules reachable from the file, when none has a tsc error; the file's own diagnostics as LSP errors with their PTS code and source `pts`; a panic in the checks becomes one warning instead of ending the server).
- **Diagnostics** carry `Start` and `End` offsets (`pts/diagnostic`), set by `diagnostic.At` (the node's token start and end) and `FromTS`, so the editor underlines what they are about.
- **Upstream edits** (`docs/FORK.md`): the two hooks; `ptslib:///` URIs kept as they are (`lsconv.FileNameToDocumentURI`, `DocumentUri.FileName`); `.pts` in the project service's relevant file names and in the file globs for new files and renames; `ptslib:///` files not watched.
- **Tests:** `pts/lsp/lsp_test.go` (in process, over `lsptestutil`'s pipes: a `.pts` file with `parallel for`, `u8` and `pts:parallel` gets no tsc errors and gets PTS2002 and PTS3001 at their ranges; tsc's errors come first and hold back the PTS checks; go to definition of `range` reaches `ptslib:///parallel.pts`), `TestLSPServesOverStdio` (`cmd/pts`: initialize answered, exit code 0 after shutdown and exit; an unknown option is a usage error).

### 9.116 The VS Code extension (M7.2)

- **Extension** (D-169, `pts/editors/vscode`): `src/extension.ts` starts a vscode-languageclient `LanguageClient` (id `parallelscript`, so `parallelscript.trace.server` traces it) over `<parallelscript.server.path> lsp --stdio` for `file:` and `untitled:` documents of the language `parallelscript` (`.pts`), pulling diagnostics on change and save; the command `parallelscript.restartServer`; a restart when the path setting changes; one output channel that every client writes to; an error message naming the setting when the path isn't an executable (the client reports other failures to start). `scripts/bundle.mjs` bundles it with esbuild (CommonJS, Node 20, `vscode` external, minified) and writes `dist/ThirdPartyNotices.txt` from the bundle's metafile. `.vscodeignore` ships only the manifest, README, LICENSE, language configuration, grammar and `dist/`.
- **Grammar:** `scripts/grammar.mjs` makes `syntaxes/ParallelScript.tmLanguage.json` (git-ignored, built by `npm run grammar`) from `syntaxes/TypeScript.tmLanguage.json` (VS Code 1.141.0's, converted from TypeScript-TmLanguage 48f6086): `.ts` scope suffixes become `.pts`; the repository rule `parallel-keyword` (two matches scoped `keyword.control.parallel.pts`: `parallel` before `for`, and `parallel` before an operand's first character, `[_$[:alpha:]]`, a digit, `.` and a digit, a quote, `{` or `~`, unless the word is `as`, `satisfies`, `of`, `in`, `instanceof` or `for`; both on the same line, not after `.` or inside a longer name) is included first in `statements` and `expressionWithoutIdentifiers`.
- **Server** (upstream edits, `docs/FORK.md`): `workspace/textDocumentContent` answers for `lsp.ContentSchemes` (`ptslib`, `bundled` when embedded), reading `<scheme>:///<path>` whether or not the client kept the empty authority; the semantic tokens skip the callee of a call marked `NodeFlagsPtsParallel`.
- **Tests:** `test/grammar.test.mjs` (`npm test`, after a type check of the extension: every sample in `test/parallel_samples.json`, `for`'s scope after the keyword, `parallel(e)` as a function call, no `.ts` scope left), `pts/lsp/grammar_test.go` (the parser on the same samples), `TestLibraryFilesAreServedToTheClient` and `TestParallelKeywordHasNoSemanticToken` (`pts/lsp`), and `test/e2e/` (`npm run test:vscode`: VS Code 1.141.0 headless runs the extension against `pts-bin/pts` on a scratch workspace: the language id, PTS2002 from the server, go to definition of `range` opening `ptslib:/parallel.pts` with its text, a hover, no semantic token on the keyword). The CI job `VS Code extension` runs `make vscode` and the end-to-end suite.

### 9.117 Papercuts, help and hints (M7.3a)

- **Hints** (D-170, `pts/frontend/hints.go`): `Hint(ctx, program, d)` for TS2339 on a property access whose name is one of `count`, `sum`, `min`, `max`, `sortBy`, `toArray` and whose receiver is array-like (`IsArrayLikeType`) or one of the nine typed arrays, with a message for a receiver that is a call `….par.m(…)` (`count` after `filter` gets its own) and one for any other array; and for TS2349 at the identifier `parallel` that is the callee of a call (not marked `NodeFlagsPtsParallel`) that is itself called, when `parallel` resolves to the prelude's declaration. `errorsOf` (`pts/frontend/load.go`) appends a hint to tsc's message after `"\n  "`; `pts/lsp`'s `ptsDiagnostics` returns, for a file with tsc errors, one Information diagnostic per hint at the error's range.
- **Decimal points** (`pts/check/check.go`, `literal`): a literal typed by an integer context whose text contains `.` (not hex) is PTS2002, with one message where the nearest enclosing non-parenthesis node is an arithmetic binary expression and another elsewhere.
- **`Function`:** `unsupportedIdentifier` reports the global `Function` in a type (`ast.IsPartOfTypeNode`) as PTS1001 before the PTS1002 of `new Function`.
- **CLI** (`cmd/pts/cli.go`): `commandUsage` per command; `wantsHelp` (`-h`/`--help` anywhere for `build`, `emit-c` and `lsp`, before the file for `run`); `usageError` carries its command; `version` and `--version` print `version` with `vcs.revision`'s first nine digits (and `, modified`) from the build info, and `core.Version()`.
- **Tests:** `pts/frontend/hints_test.go` (each hint, and errors that only look alike get none: a class's `sum`, a method `.par` lacks, a program's own `parallel`), `TestTscErrorsGetParallelScriptsHints` (`pts/lsp`), `pts/tests/errors/parallel_hints.pts`, `decimal_point_integers.pts` and `function_type.pts`, and in `cmd/pts`: `TestEachCommandHasItsOwnHelp`, `TestHelpAfterRunsFileGoesToTheProgram`, `TestUsageErrorsShowTheirCommandsUsage`, `TestVersionNamesPtsAndTypeScriptGo`.

### 9.118 `pts test`, `pts:test` and `pts:assert` (M7.3b)

- **`pts:assert`** (D-171, `pts/lib/assert.pts`): the assertions over `unknown`, failing through `failWith` (an `Error` given as the message is thrown as it is); `unequalMessage` (Node's `a !== b` for two primitives that aren't both zero, "Values have same structure but are not reference-equal" when the inspections are equal, else a diff); `diffLines` (an LCS over the lines of the two inspections); `withComparison` (a message given to `equal` or `deepEqual` followed by the comparison, as Node 24 shows it); `isDeepStrictEqual` (classes compared by `pts_value_constructor_name`, then Dates, RegExps, errors, Maps and Sets through `any`, else own enumerable properties). Two intrinsics: `pts_util_inspect` (new, `console.c`: `pts_inspect` with depth 2) and `pts_value_constructor_name`.
- **`pts:test`** (`pts/lib/test.pts`): `test` and `describe` push `Test`s and `Suite`s into the current suite (`describe` runs its function at once with its suite current) and start the run with `setImmediate` once; `runSuite` runs `before` hooks, the children in order (more may be added while it runs), then `after` hooks; `runTest` runs the `beforeEach` hooks outermost first, the test, the `afterEach` hooks innermost first, and prints its line. A test's `location` is the first stack frame outside the library when it was registered. `failureReport` inspects the error and drops the library's frames. `report` prints the totals and failures, or writes them to `PTS_TEST_RESULTS` as JSON.
- **`pts test`** (`cmd/pts/test.go`): `findTestFiles`, `buildTestFiles` (`driver.Build`, at most `min(4, NumCPU/2)` at once, into a temporary directory), `runTestFile` (the executable with the CLI's stdio and `PTS_TEST_RESULTS`; a file that didn't build or left no results is one failed test), `printTestSummary`.
- **Compiler fixes found by writing them:** `x instanceof Map` and `x instanceof Set` (`ValueIsMap`, `ValueIsSet`: `pts_value_is_map`, `pts_value_is_set`) were PTS1001; a `for…of` over a dynamic value narrowed to a Map by tsc (a variable declared `any` or `unknown`) crashed the compiler, as the lowering took tsc's narrowed Map type for the value's representation (`storedDynamic` keeps such values dynamic, and `isNarrowedDynamicCollection` reads and calls their members dynamically: `value.size`, `value.get(k)`); an error class used as a value says how to test an error's class.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/assert_module.pts` (against Node's `node:assert/strict`: the differential tests now map `pts:assert` to it), `run/instanceof_collections.pts` (against Node; `errors/inheritance_not_yet.pts` now pins `instanceof Array` as not supported, `instanceof Map` being supported), `cmd/pts/test_test.go` (a project with nested suites, hooks, skips, todos, failures and files that `pts test` must not find; a test file run on its own; files that don't compile or end early; no test files; the guide's sample, `docs/examples/testing/`), `TestTestModifiersGetAHint` (`pts/frontend`), `errors/error_class_values.pts`.

### 9.119 Papercuts from two programs written from scratch (M7.3c)

- **Loose locals** (D-172, `pts/lower/loose_locals.go`): `isLoose` holds for a local whose initializer reads an element (or that an array pattern binds) when the program tests it for undefined or its declared type holds undefined; `optionalChain` widens its result with undefined when its object is a loose identifier, as for an element read.
- **Hash-map reads** (`records.go`, `recordRead`): a property or element read of a hash-map object whose entry type is a union with undefined (or `any`) uses `MapGet` (undefined when missing) and converts to the entry type, both paths narrowed afterwards; other entry types keep `MapGetChecked`.
- **Numbers** (`pts/check/check.go`): `isNullableNumber` (one numeric type with null or undefined) routes such expressions through `contextualExpr` and types them by their numeric member, without recording a type for the expression itself; `nullishLeft` types `??`'s left that way; `pair` gives a branch next to a nullish one the expected type. In lowering, `takesContextualUnion` makes `?:` use its context's union for an empty array literal and for an integer next to null or undefined; `impliedType` gives an object literal in a branch of `?:` without a context the other branch's object type; `emptyArrayAs` converts an empty array literal to a union's one array member.
- **Async arrows** (`closures.go`, `yield_expressions.go`): `functionExpressionBody` lowers an expression body with suspensions through `hoistedExpression` (`hoistYields`, then the expression), sharing `forgetHoisted` with `hoistingStatement`.
- **Messages:** `identifier` (`lower.go`) reports the ES error classes and the program's classes used as values with the `instanceof` advice; PTS3001 and PTS3002's `await` (`pts/check/parallel.go`); `withArticle` in PTS2001 and PTS2002; `parallelViewHint` (`pts/frontend/hints.go`).
- **Runtime:** `add_source_frame` (`error.c`) leaves out `.S` and `.s` files.
- **Tests:** `run/declared_undefined_locals.pts`, `run/conditional_empty_literals.pts`, `run/async_arrow_expression_bodies.pts` (against Node), `run/nullish_integers.pts`, `TestParallelViewsOfWhatHasNoneGetAHint`, `errors/error_class_values.pts` (a program class too); the pinned messages of seven error tests and two `pts/check` tests updated for "an int", and `errors/any_not_yet.pts` for the class message.

### 9.120 Logical assignments on dynamic places (H02)

- **Lowering** (D-176): `compoundAssignment` (`lower.go`) sends `??=` and `||=` on a hash-map entry to `recordElementOrSet` and on a dynamic place (`place.dynamic`) through `readAndStore`, as `&&=`; `nullishAssignment` (`unions.go`) tests dynamic places (`isDynamic`); `logicalAssignmentValue` converts the value of `??=`, `||=` and `&&=` (from `compoundAssignment` and `recordElementOrSet`), unless `isDiscarded`, with `checkedConversion` to `logicalAssignmentType`: tsc's type where it is the place's type or narrows it, else the place's type (`withoutNullish` where `storesNonNullish`); `compoundAssignment` converts `||=`'s and `&&=`'s stored value to the narrowed read's type, or tests the unnarrowed read where the value is discarded; `recordEntry` (`records.go`) reads an entry for `recordRead` and for `place.read` (`place.at` and `place.l` are set for entries); `recordKey` converts dynamic keys with `ToString`; `recordFallback` tests `any` entries as it tests unions.
- **Runtime:** `pts_dynamic_set` (`dynamic.c`) returns the value `pts_storable` stored, from `object_set` for fields and setters and directly for array elements and record entries.
- **Tests:** `run/logical_assign_erased.pts`, `run/logical_assign_any.pts`, `run/logical_assign_any_places.pts`, `run/logical_assign_narrowed.pts`, `run/logical_assign_typed_values.pts`, `run/record_any_entries.pts`, `run/record_erased_keys.pts`, `run/dynamic_write_value.pts` (against Node), `run/logical_assign_any_checks.pts` (the checked writes' messages); `run/logical_assign_suspensions.pts` (C25, H08) and `run/keyed_assign_value.pts` (C26, H04) are skipped known issues.

### 9.121 Lazy iteration, void getters and bare returns (H03a)

- **IR** (D-177): `ir.IterationOpen`, `IterationNext`, `IterationValue`, `IterationRest`, `IterationClose` replace `ValueIterable`, `ValueIterableLength`, `ValueIterableElement` and `IteratorTake`.
- **Lowering:** `forOfStatement` (`arrays.go`) sends dynamic values to `iteratorLoop` (`generators.go`, split from `iteratorForOf`), which steps an `IterationOpen` source by index (`IterationNext`, `IterationValue`) and closes it with `iteratorClosing`; `destructure` sends array patterns over dynamic values and iterable objects (`isIterablePatternSource`) to `destructureIterable` (`destructuring.go`), which steps per element (`IterationRest` for a rest element) and, when a binding may throw (`isPlainBinding`), declares its names ahead and binds them inside `withFinally`; `bindElement` binds a name or a nested pattern; `readsElement` (`loose_locals.go`) makes a declaration's pattern names loose over any source but a tuple, and `bindName` converts a pattern's dynamic item to a type without an in-place check, or a union with one, once at the binding (`patternItem`; not an element read from an array with a declared element type, `isTypedElementRead`), and `looseRead` reads a loose 64-bit integer exactly; `compoundAssignment` concatenates `+=` onto a loose string's narrowed read, with a right side's `valueOf` as `+` does; `forStatement` gives a `let` initializer's names per-iteration copies (`declaredNames`, replacing `declaredLocals`). `resultType` (`lower.go`) gives `void` and `never` getters the result `undefined`; `returnStatement` converts undefined for a bare `return;` where the result isn't `void`. `reportedGetterResult` (`objects.go`) explains a failed conversion of a literal with a `done` or `value` accessor to an iterator result (`isIteratorResultType`, `union_objects.go`) in `objectCast`, `classCast` (`classes.go`) and `toUnion` (`unions.go`).
- **Runtime** (`generator.c`): `pts_iteration_open` (an iterator for generators, runtime iterators, maps, sets and objects with `@@iterator`, a typed array itself, else `pts_value_iterable`'s items), `pts_iteration_next`, `pts_iteration_value`, `pts_iteration_rest`, `pts_iteration_close`; `pts_iterator_open` makes live map and set iterators (`collection_iterator`, as `pts_iterator_get` does); `pts_iterator_take` and `pts_value_iterable_length`/`_element` (`dynamic_methods.c`) are gone; `pts_value_to_number_checked` is inline in `pts.h` over `pts_value_to_number_slow` (`string.c`); `convert.c`'s cycle stacks are initialised by their depth only, not zero-filled.
- **Tests:** `run/getter_void_never.pts`, `run/getter_void_interface.pts`, `run/return_bare_undefined.pts`, `run/any_iteration_lazy.pts`, `run/array_pattern_lazy.pts`, `run/iteration_unbounded_lazy.pts`, `run/iterable_collections_live.pts` (the reproducer's), `run/iterator_result_getters_any.pts`, `run/iterator_result_getters_inferred.pts`, `run/array_pattern_abrupt.pts`, `run/array_pattern_past_end.pts`, `run/for_let_pattern_iterables.pts`, `run/typed_array_iteration_live.pts`, `run/array_pattern_loose_identity.pts`, `run/array_pattern_loose_concat.pts`, `run/array_pattern_union_items.pts`, `run/pattern_union_dynamic_items.pts`, `run/compound_concat_valueof.pts` (against Node), `run/array_pattern_loose_int64.pts`, `run/array_pattern_checked_close.pts`, `errors/iterator_result_getters.pts` (removed by H03b, §9.122); `run/iterator_result_getters_typed.pts` (C03), `run/iterator_next_result_getters.pts` (C30, H03b) and `run/iterator_result_as_cast.pts` (C35, H03b) were skipped known issues (they run since H03b, §9.122).

### 9.122 Iterator results as the objects iterators hand back (H03b)

- **IR** (D-178): `ir.Class.Result` marks D-109's result shapes, whose values may be objects of any layout, and `ir.Interface.Result` the interface of a property of a union with a result among its members; `ir.ClassCast.FromResult` converts a result of another layout to an interface; `ir.Yield.Delegate` is a `yield*`'s step; the builtins `IteratorCall`, `IteratorHanded` and `IteratorCallDone`.
- **Lowering:** `iteratorResultShape` (`generators.go`) marks the shapes (and a program's shape with the same fields); `iteratorMethodCall` takes `it.return!(v)`'s callee too and calls the iterator (`IteratorCall`), giving the object its method returned as it is or a shape made of what a runtime iterator reports; `delegation` yields `Delegate`; `asResult` (`unions.go`) keeps an object of the program's as a result from `classCast`, `objectCast`, `toUnion` and `asUnion` (`isResultUnion`, `isObjectOfProgram`); `interfaceCast` (also for D-066's relaxed casts) and `convertedResult` (a conditional's branches, after `commonClass` and `asContextualInterface`) (`objects.go`) mark casts from a result to an interface `FromResult`; `readAndStore` (`lower.go`) reads a result's property once for a read-modify-write, and `readKeptAndStore` uses D-145's read kept before a `yield` on the right side, converted to the place's type, instead of reading again; `delegation` binds the `yield*`'s value to a local after its loop; `holdsIteratorResults` sends a union's property to `resultUnionProperty` (`union_objects.go`); `prepareDynamicTypes` (`dynamic.go`) readies results' property types (`firstResult`); `reportedGetterResult` and its PTS1001 are gone, and `interfaceCast` reports a PTS1001 for an interface that can't take a result's conversion.
- **C emitter:** `resultRead`, `resultStore` and `resultValue` (`classes.go`) read and write a result's `done` and `value` as the shape's fields if `isShapeObject` (any of the shape's property orders, D-108) and by name otherwise; `fromValue` (`cgen.go`) keeps an object of the program's for a result type, maybe with `undefined` or `null` (`isResultType`); the check of a result shape has `.result = true` (`checks.go`); `classCast` converts a `FromResult` cast's object of another layout with the interface's check; `yieldStatement` (`generators.go`) writes `pts_generator_delegate` for a `Delegate`; `collectChecks` and `effectsOf` know the new reads, writes and casts.
- **Runtime:** `pts_result_get` (inline, a site cache) and `pts_result_get_slow` (a field, a getter, or a class's method read as through `any`; undefined without allocating if there is none), `pts_result_set` (`pts.h`, `object.c`); `pts_iterator.result`, the result object a step handed back, kept per thread with its value (`set_step`, `last_step`, D-143); `pts_iterator_call` (`call_object_method`, whose TypeError names the call's text, as for `return`/`throw` on the runtime's iterators), `pts_iterator_handed`, `pts_iterator_call_done`, `pts_generator_delegate`, `pts_iterator_method_call` (`generator.c`); an object iterator's step keeps the result and reads its `value` when asked (`handed_value`), and `pts_iterator_close` reads nothing of `return()`'s result; `pts_builtin_method_call` (`dynamic_methods.c`) calls iterators' methods, and arrays', maps' and sets' `values`, `keys` and `entries` make their runtime iterators (`pts_array_iterator_new`); `pts_iterator_result_new` and `pts_value_is_async_generator` (`promise.c`) are shared; `pts_check.result`, which `convert` (`convert.c`) honours by keeping any of the program's objects, in a union only after its other members failed (`results_last`), and which `implies` never lets imply another object type's check.
- **Tests:** `run/iterator_methods_through_any.pts`, `run/iterator_next_result_identity.pts`, `run/iterator_return_throw_results.pts`, `run/yield_star_result_passthrough.pts`, `run/array_pattern_elision_done_only.pts`, `run/iterator_result_as_layouts.pts`, `run/iterator_result_as_one_shape.pts` (the reproducer's), `run/iterator_result_getter_literals.pts`, `run/yield_star_result_edges.pts`, `run/iterator_close_result_unread.pts`, `run/iterators_through_any_consumers.pts`, `run/iterator_results_from_any.pts`, `run/iterator_results_other_types.pts` (against Node), `run/iterator_results_threads.pts` (on 1, 2 and 4 threads), `run/iterator_results_copied.pts` (`no-diff`: pins D-071's copies); `run/iterator_result_getters_typed.pts`, `run/iterator_next_result_getters.pts` and `run/iterator_result_as_cast.pts` run; `run/any_maps_error.pts` expects map iterators through `any` to work.

### 9.123 Compiler failure containment (H04)

- **Diagnostics** (D-179): `diagnostic.InternalError` (a `Diagnostic` with code `Internal`, `PTS9001`; `Detail`, the Go stack or the C compiler's output; `Earlier`, the phase's errors before it), `Locate` (deferred by a phase: a panic becomes a located internal error, panicked with), `Contain` (deferred by `driver.EmitC`: returns it), `WithInjectedFailure`/`InjectedFailure` (tests' context value); `Diagnostic.Format` leaves out an unknown column (0).
- **Phases:** `check.Program` defers `Locate` with `pass.at`, the innermost node `visit` has entered (restored on return; a panic leaves it); `lower.Program` with `lowerer.current`, set by `lowering` in `statement` (whose body is now `statementOf`) and `expression` and per statement or class in `declareClasses`, `declareSuperclasses`, `declareFunctions`, `declareModuleBindings` and `declareClassMembers`; `cgen.Emit` with `out.source`, the last position `at` was given. The driver's `generatedCFailure` turns a `toolchain.CommandError` of the program's compile into an internal error by clang's first `error:` (`cCompilerError`) or the linker's `undefined reference` (`undefinedReference`).
- **CLI:** `exitInternal` (3), `cli.debug` (`PTS_DEBUG=1`), `reportInternalError` (also for `pts test`); the usage lists both. The language server's `internalError` shows a checks' internal error at its node in the file.
- **Lowering** (D-179): `assignmentValue` (the value of `=` and of an arithmetic or string `op=`), `assignedValue`/`assignedValueType` (D-176's `logicalAssignmentValue`/`logicalAssignmentType`; `storesNonNullish` knows `=`), `storedDynamic` (members of narrowed dynamic values, their methods' results, `x!`, `=` on a dynamic place); `narrowedValue` converts a typed value held dynamically to an array, tuple, record, map or set type (`dynamicConversion`) and leaves a stored dynamic one (`isContainerType`, `isNarrowedDynamic`, which takes interfaces too, `isDynamicCollection`), whose members `narrowedMember` reads (undefined where a context takes it) and whose property reads, element reads (`valueExpression` and `nullishCoalescing` loosely), writes, method calls (their results narrowed), `apply` (`callArguments`), `Object.keys` (`objectStaticCall`) and array iteration (`isArrayIteration`) go the dynamic way, `isArrayExpression` and `isRecordExpression` leaving it out; `deleteExpression` reports a dynamic value's `delete` as PTS1001; `copyShape` passes over object literals' types with accessors; `literalMemberName`, `accessorFields` and `accessorsKey` by `propertyName` (whether each accessor property has a getter, a setter or both); `spreadNames` returns `ok`; `destructure` (an unexplained failure of `destructureParts`); `restTarget` and `assignParts` for a `SpreadAssignment`; `methodParamTypes` (interface methods and erased generic methods' signatures).
- **Runtime:** `pts_iterator_method` (GetMethod of `@@iterator`, read once, a non-function a TypeError through `call_iterator_method`; `pts.h`, `generator.c`), used by `pts_iterator_get`, `pts_iterator_open`, `pts_iteration_open` and `pts_value_iterable` (`dynamic_methods.c`); `pts_async_iterator_open` calls the `@@asyncIterator` it read (`promise.c`).
- **Tests:** `pts/diagnostic/internal_test.go`, `TestFormatOmitsAnUnknownColumn`; `TestAPanicWhileEmittingIsAnInternalErrorAtItsStatement` (cgen, a hand-made IR); `TestAPanicWhileLowering…`, `TestAnInternalErrorWhileLoweringKeepsTheErrorsReportedBeforeIt` (lower), `TestAPanicInTheChecksIsAnInternalErrorAtTheInnermostNodeVisited` (check), `TestAPanicWhileCompilingIsReturnedAsAnInternalError` and `generated_c_test.go` (driver, a real clang: rejected C at its `#line`, before any `#line`, an undefined reference), `TestAnInternalCompilerErrorIsReportedAsABugWithItsOwnExitCode`, `TestPTSDebugAddsTheCompilersStackToAnInternalError`, `TestAnInternalCompilerErrorFollowsTheErrorsFoundBeforeIt`, `TestTestReportsATestFileTheCompilerFailsOn` (`cmd/pts`), `internal_error_test.go` (`pts/lsp`); `TestCompilerFailsCleanly` (`pts/tests/compiler_smoke_test.go`: every run, errors, corpus and docs program and `PTS_SMOKE_MUTANTS` fixed-seed mutants each, default 1, compiled in process; `PTS_SMOKE_CLANG=1`, `PTS_SMOKE_SEED`, `PTS_SMOKE_FILTER`, `PTS_SMOKE_JOBS`, `PTS_SMOKE_OUT`); run tests against Node: `keyed_assign_value.pts` (unskipped), `keyed_assign_value_types.pts`, `dynamic_assign_value_types.pts`, `assign_value_union_places.pts`, `narrowed_tuples_of_dynamic_unions.pts`, `narrowed_arrays_of_dynamic_unions.pts`, `narrowed_records_of_dynamic_unions.pts`, `narrowed_dynamic_values.pts`, `object_literal_iterator_accessors.pts`, `iterator_getter_not_a_function.pts`, `object_literal_accessor_shapes.pts`, `object_rest_empty.pts`, `object_rest_assignment.pts`, `interface_method_tuple_rest.pts`; `narrowed_dynamic_data_copies.pts` and `object_rest_static_type.pts` (`no-diff`: pin D-071's copies at typed places and D-058's static-type rest); `errors/symbol_iterator_element_writes.pts`, `errors/dynamic_delete.pts`; `TestAnUndefinedReferenceInTheRuntimeIsNotACompilerBug` (driver); `TestAPanicAfter…` (lower, check: positions restored after children).

### 9.124 Sort, toSorted, `.par.sort` and the copying methods (H06a)

- **Runtime** (D-182, `array.c`): `sort_place_of` (compared, undefined or a hole), `sorted_tail`, `sort_string` (ToString of a compared element: `null` is `"null"`, the rest `element_string`'s), `pts_array_sort` and `pts_array_sort_with` (each slot read once; the compared elements sorted, then the undefineds and the holes written back to the buffer as it is after the user calls; the frozen rule: two or more slots, not holes only), `pts_array_copy_as_read` (holes of a dynamic-value array as undefined), used by `pts_array_with` and the dynamic path's `copy_of` (`dynamic_methods.c`), `pts_array_gather_sortable` (on a copy; nothing to do for arrays of numbers, booleans and the integer kinds) and `pts_array_write_sorted`, with their `pts_parallel_` intrinsics.
- **C emitter:** `ir.ArrayCopy` is `pts_array_copy_as_read` (`arrays.go`).
- **Lowering:** `sortWith` (`arrays.go`) takes a receiver's `sortSteps` (`arraySortSteps`; `typedSortWith`'s in `typed_arrays.go`: the typed array's own sort by default, and with a comparator its elements copied to a `number[]` after the comparator is bound) and decides by the comparator's lowered type: an `ir.UndefinedConst` or `ir.Undefined` is the default order, a union with undefined or a dynamic value a run-time test (`IsNullish`) after the receiver and the comparator are bound, any other a conversion to `(T, T) => number`; `withCopy` copies for `toSorted` after the argument; `toSpliced` (`array_library.go`) binds the receiver and the operands that aren't constants before copying.
- **Library** (`pts/lib/parallel.pts`): `ParallelArray.sort` is Array's sort on one thread and otherwise sorts a copy (`items.slice()`, gathered, sorted with `sortInPlace` or by the keys' `permute`) and writes it back; `sortInPlace` takes a count; `compareStrings` replaces `compareDefault`; `permute` writes `order.length` items.
- **Tests:** `array_test.c` (`sort_puts_undefined_after_the_others_and_holes_last`, `sort_keeps_holes_of_strings_last`, `sort_with_never_passes_undefined_or_holes`, `a_copy_as_read_makes_holes_undefined`, `gather_moves_the_compared_elements_to_the_front`, `write_sorted_stores_every_slot_unless_frozen`); `run/sort_undefined_last.pts`, `run/sort_null_strings.pts`, `run/sort_holes.pts`, `run/sort_comparator_never_undefined.pts`, `run/tosorted_holes.pts`, `run/sort_any_arrays.pts`, `run/sort_optional_comparator.pts`, `run/sort_typed_array_undefined_comparator.pts`, `run/sort_dynamic_comparator.pts`, `run/sort_undefined_spellings.pts`, `run/sort_tostring_grows.pts`, `run/sort_frozen_arrays.pts`, `run/copying_methods_argument_order.pts`, `run/array_copies_read_holes.pts` (against Node), `run/parallel_sort_undefined.pts`, `run/parallel_sort_holes.pts`, `run/parallel_sort_all_or_nothing.pts` (on 1, 2 and 4 threads, expected output from sequential Node twins); H06b's and H22's RED tests are skipped known issues: `run/array_callbacks_skip_holes.pts`, `any_array_callbacks_skip_holes`, `array_find_holes`, `array_search_undefined_holes`, `array_keys_skip_holes`, `array_in_skips_holes`, `array_iteration_reads_holes`, `any_concat_keeps_holes`, `match_spreads_keep_undefined` (C74, C78, C88–C92), `array_conversion_keeps_holes` (C93).

### 9.125 The array built-ins at holes (H06b)

- **IR** (D-183): `ir.HoldsUndefined` (any or unknown, undefined, a union with undefined); `ArrayElementOrUndefined` (JS's Get of an array whose element type holds undefined, or with `As` of an array of pointers read as a wider type: undefined for a hole or an index out of range), `ArrayHas` (HasProperty) and `ArraySpread` (a spread's array: concat's shape, each array's items as iterating reads them).
- **C emitter** (`arrays.go`, `maps.go`): `readsHoles` decides by the static element type: `ArrayCopy` is `pts_array_copy_as_read` or `pts_array_copy`, `ArrayWith` passes `pts_array_with` the flag, `ArraySpread` appends with `pts_array_append_as_read`; `ArrayHas` is `pts_array_has_word` for arrays of pointers and dynamic values; a set of an array passes `pts_set_of_array` whether the set holds undefined.

H07b's skipped RED tests: `run/abort_signal_shared_parallel.pts` (C116), `run/abort_timeouts_sleeping_loops.pts` (C120), `run/abort_signal_any_order.pts` (C121).

### 9.127 Signals shared by threads (H07b1)

- **Library** (D-185, `pts/lib/internal/abort.pts`): `EventTarget`'s `#lock` (a 4-byte ArrayBuffer for `pts_sync_lock`/`pts_sync_unlock`, reached from AbortSignal through `lockOf`, which EventTarget's static block sets); `#add`, `#listeners` (a dispatch's snapshot) and `#take` (a `once` listener's removal) work under it. AbortSignal's `#dependents` (made by the first follower, handed over when the signal aborts) and `#sources` (a signal `any` made: the sources it follows); `#follow` (adds a dependent unless the source has aborted, returning the source's reason), `#settle` (decides the abort, sets the reason, hands over the dependents; a frozen signal's TypeError puts the reason back and releases the lock) and `#claimHandler`; `signalAbort` settles the signal, then each dependent, then fires the signal's event and the dependents', and throws a frozen dependent's TypeError last. `addEventListener` adds the `{ signal }` option's listener first (through `#append`, with no duplicate scan; used up by a flag of its own; `resists`, a registration's flag that keeps it called after `stopImmediatePropagation`), then the listener, then checks the signal again through `abortedNow`. `#settle` checks `Object.isFrozen` before it changes anything. `pts/lib/http.pts`: `OWN_SIGNAL_LOCK` and `Request`'s `#ownSignal`, which `disconnect` pairs with; `exchange` re-checks `signal.aborted` after adding its `EventListener`.
- **Tests:** `run/abort_signal_shared_parallel.pts` and `run/abort_signal_shared_threads.pts` (under 1, 2 and 4 threads: followers from `.par.map`, `parallel for` and `parallel` tasks; followers made while another thread aborts the signal, and one against an abort a moment later; the program's own listeners added from threads; one controller aborted from two threads at once; `onabort` set from two threads at once; a follower's two sources aborted on two threads at once; removes against adds; `{ signal }` while another thread aborts it, five times, and one listener with `{ signal }` against an abort a moment later; a `once` listener with two threads dispatching), `run/fetch_abort_other_thread.pts` (under 1, 2 and 4 threads, a server that never answers: aborts landing in each step of a fetch's start on another thread, and 200 `.par.map` fetches sharing a signal), `run/abort_signal_any_cost.pts` (500,000 followers in about a second; minutes on the base), `run/abort_signal_any_order.pts` and `run/abort_signal_any_dependents.pts` (against Node: order, flattening, duplicates, two sources, aborted inputs, reasons, `onabort`, aborts inside a dispatch, a timeout source), `run/abort_listener_signal_option.pts` (against Node: the `{ signal }` option on the signal itself, on a listener added twice, an aborted signal, with `once`, after an earlier listener stops the abort event, and an untrusted `abort` event), `run/abort_signal_frozen.pts` (a frozen signal's and a frozen follower's abort), `run/abort_listener_signal_option_cost.pts` (100,000 listeners given one signal, added and removed by its abort in under a second). Their Node twins are in `docs/agent/hardening-evidence/probes/h07b/oracles/`.

- **Runtime** (D-186): `scheduler.c`: the slots' condition variables wait on `CLOCK_MONOTONIC`; `wait_for_work` sleeps until a message or, through `wait_at_most`, the loop's first timer (`pts_loop_timer_wait`); `rejoin` raises `awake` only above zero; `run` starts with one `UV_RUN_DEFAULT` pass, flushes stdout before a loop sleeps, runs `pts_loop_run_due_timers` after waking (before the inbox), and after that work or a stolen task goes on with one `UV_RUN_NOWAIT` pass, its own run of the due timers if that pass leaves nothing referenced, and `UV_RUN_ONCE` passes while something is, so immediates run before the next timers; `own_index` and `pts_own_loop` (the thread's own loop); `pts_parallel_nesting` and `pts_parallel_on_main_thread`. `loop.c`: `now_of` (libuv's time read afresh) for `pts_timer_set`, `refresh`, an interval's next run (taken before its callback) and `arm`'s wait (which takes the caller's reading); a timer's `loop` is `pts_own_loop()`; `pts_loop_init` takes the thread's loop before it allocates; `pts_loop_timer_wait`, `pts_loop_run_due_timers`. `http.c`: a server's listeners count from `pts_own_loop()`. `promise.c`'s `run_task` and `pool.c`'s `operate` raise and lower `pts_parallel_nesting`, also on their throw paths.
- **Library:** `pts/lib/test.pts`: `requireMainThread`, called first by `test`, `it`, `describe`, `suite` (`it` and `suite` are functions of their own now, so the error names them), `before`, `after`, `beforeEach` and `afterEach`; `addTest`, `addSuite`.
- **Tests:** `run/abort_timeouts_sleeping_loops.pts` (unskipped), `run/timers_unref_sleeping_loops.pts` (a task's unref'd interval and its clear from another thread, a task's timer unref'd and one refreshed from another thread, cleared ones never firing while later ones do, the main loop's unref'd timer while a task works) and `run/timers_unref_program_end.pts` (a guard: only unreferenced timers left, the program ends at once; one ref'd from another thread keeps it running), `run/timers_after_sleep.pts` and `run/timers_count_from_when_set.pts` (against Node: timers set after work or a sleep don't fire early, and keep their order), `run/timers_par_callbacks.pts` (timers set in `.par` callbacks of four tasks' operations, unref'd, ref'd, refreshed and cleared from the main thread), `run/serve_par_callbacks.pts` (servers started there, fetched from and stopped), `run/timers_refused_no_leak.pts` (3.2 million callbacks, a few hundred thousand of whose timers are refused, grow the process by under 25 MB), all but `timers_count_from_when_set` under several thread counts; `run/pts_test_registry_threads.pts` (every registration function from tasks, `.par.map`, `.par.forEach`, `parallel for` and `join`, and the error's text), `cmd/pts/test_test.go`'s `TestTestRegistrationOffTheMainThreadThrows` (a file whose suite body starts tasks and an operation that try to register, under 1, 2 and 4 threads), and `pts/runtime/tests/scheduler_test.c` (sleeping loops' unreferenced timers, intervals and 200 clears racing their wakes, under ASan/UBSan and TSan). Their Node twins are in `docs/agent/hardening-evidence/probes/h07b2/oracles/`.

### 9.137 A narrower fallback; emit-c prints the C a build compiles (H09b5b)

H09b5b is an implementation checkpoint; independent acceptance is blocked by a provider content refusal ([exact error and disposition](agent/hardening-evidence/h09b5b-resume-review.md)).

- **Resumed refinements** (D-200): `pairing.signature` (`demandIdentity`: instantiated checker type arguments, parameters and result, cached per demand), `pairing.owner` (`substitutionIdentity`: target outer parameter bindings, including erased parameters); narrowing identity stable across relowerings of the same checked program, not an IR/runtime key. `blameOf` wraps `findBlame` and validates a proposed substitution cut with `survives` (linear forward-worklist least fixed point; any surviving user AND an existing enclosing class); an unproved cut conservatively falls back, before `relaxedCastUse` can turn it into plain pairings. `useFrom`, `instanceUse`, `useListedInstance` record later descriptor hits in `specializeValue` and `specializeMethod`. `trial.root` (set by `genericInstanceFor`) separates the trial’s report ownership from its signature/body dependencies’ instance ownership, so descriptor reuses keep all dependencies. Tests: `lower/narrowing_test.go` (deterministic outer cut, independent fixed-seed 10,000-graph oracle, descriptor/dependency uses) and `run/generic_fallback_spares_other_{instances_at_one_call,class_instances_at_one_call,cast_instances_at_one_call,method_owners}.pts`, `generic_fallback_blames_descriptor_reuses.pts`, `generic_fallback_blames_method_descriptor_reuses.pts` (the last is a passing-before-fix control); all six run tests compare against Node. C250 and general C251 remain open; site keys still lack per-instance identity.

- **Lowering** (D-199): `narrowing.go` (new): `pairing` (a demand's call and name with an erased value's or generic method's declaration, or a call site of a `siteKind`: `byNameSite`, `constantKeySite`, `demandSite`; with its `level`, the last of `Fallbacks` that makes it: `pairingOf`, `demand.level`, `valueLevel`, `siteLevels`), `lower.Narrowing` (`leavesOut`, `with`, `Within`, `Len`), `use` (a trial's or call sites' pairings, or an instance's substitution, or the program's own), `here` (`lowerer.site`; `lowerer.statementSites`, kept per statement by `statementOrTrap` and added to by `usedSite`; the trial being run, for its own code; the first substitution out with uses), `lowerer.use` (recorded on `substitution.users`, `used`, `ownUse` where `genericInstanceFor`, `classInstanceOf` and `erasedInstance` make or find an instance; `substitution.outer`, a class instance's method's class instance), `blameOf` (one search per report over `blames`, each substitution once, least-fixed-point across instances that make each other, `maxBlameSteps`), `blame` (`with`, `merge`, `level`), `reported` (`reportedSoFar`, `restoreReported`, which `quietly`, `tentatively`/`takeBack`, `irTypeKeyPart`, `tryInstance` and `contextualFuncType` use; `lowerer.errorUses`, `noteUse`), `narrower` (from `ProgramWith`, which takes and returns a `*Narrowing`), `blamingSite`, `leavesOutSite`, `classUse`. `report` is `reportIn` with `here`; `noteUnexplained` records `unexplainedUse`; `inFunction` records `funcUses`. Sites: `keyedInstanceCall` (around `demandAt` and from `passedAsIs` on; its demand `allOnly`), `constantKeyCall` (its dotted call; its demands for the call `narrowable`), `siteDemandAt` and `instanceCall` (for `instanceValueCall` and `dispatchedInstanceCall`, where the demand has a rest parameter or a dynamic result); each records `usedSite` where it lowered its demand. `checkRelaxedCasts` reports a cast with `relaxedCastUse`: the cast's `use`, or `objectBlame` (the code that makes the failing objects' class, `classUse`, or converts them to an interface on the way: `interfaceCasts`, now a method returning each `interfaceSource`'s uses), whichever leaves out fewer later fallbacks' instances. `trial.pairing` (from `tryInstance`'s new parameter); `tryInstance` lowers what a failed or foreign trial leaves pending outside the next trial (`lowerPending`); `specializeValue` and `specializeMethod` skip what the narrowing leaves out; `keyedInstanceCall`, `keyedCall`, `constantKeyCall` and `siteDemandAt` lower a left-out site as the first later fallback that makes no such demand does.
- **Driver** (`driver.go`): `compileC` (EmitC's and Build's one loop over `lower.Fallbacks`, each `narrowedAt`, then `accept`), `lowering`, `narrowedAt` (up to `maxLowerings` lowerings at a level, never leaving out what `laterLowering`, the first later fallback that lowers the program, keeps: `Narrowing.Within`), `checkC` (emit-c's accept: `toolchain.CheckProgram`, `clang -fsyntax-only` with the release flags and the runtime's headers), `build`, `writeC`, `rejectedC` (from `generatedCFailure`: the C compiler's rejection, which falls back; a link failure doesn't), `EmitCNarrowedWith`.
