### 9.138 Source-proven ordinary function-value pairing (H09b5c1)

- **Lowering** (D-201): `instanceValueCall` records the actual callee expression in `demand.source`; `specializeValue` calls `sourceMayReach` before making an instance. `value_flow.go` caches conservative declaration sets (`functionSources`; nil means unknown) through function expressions/declarations, const initializers, conditional/logical merges and comma RHS. `parameterSources` joins all arguments of closed non-generic named declarations only for unassigned ordinary parameters, with no default/rest/pattern ambiguity. `functionValueUses` indexes source references once, including exported/imported/aliased/shorthand uses and writes. Declaration identities conservatively stand for all enclosing generic instances. Parameter joins are cached by declaration so separate reads do not rescan all callers. Analysis is bounded (depth 128, 4,096 uncached-node/caller-join steps per query, 256 merged sources); cycles or exhaustion keep the old compatible pairing. `readSymbol` distinguishes a shorthand name from its default initializer, so a consumer escaping through `({ alias = consume } = options)` remains unknown. Methods, forwarders and their synthetic demands remain conservative; no narrowing/fallback ordering or checked-any conversion changes.
- **Tests:** `driver/speculative_instances_test.go` activates the escaping-unused regression and checks both required and excluded declaration/arrow/alias/closed-parameter/expression pairs, plus conservative escaped/exported consumer retention. `lower/value_flow_test.go` pins unknown-vs-empty, bounded unions, depth/step limits, declaration-shared caller proofs, cached-argument join budgets, caller cycles and shorthand-default escapes. Run/Node fixtures `generic_value_flow_{expression_sources,unknown_origins,unknown_parameters,shorthand_default_escape,proven_declaration}.pts` preserve evaluation order, mutated/captured/destructured parameters, escaped consumers (including destructuring defaults), generic contexts, return/container/property sources and any readback. The proven-declaration control pins the original `useDogs(make)` program that now compiles; `errors/generic_fallback_keeps_speculative_declaration_failures.pts` keeps its unchanged diagnostic assertion with a genuinely unknown any[] argument instead of that obsolete known-source precondition. The existing driver's fallback test now uses a genuinely unknown `any[]` readback: its former `use(make)` proved the unrelated failing arrow unreachable, so its forced-AllInstances-error precondition was obsolete; its expected diagnostic/fallback assertions are unchanged. Nine former `needsNarrowing` smoke exemptions were removed because their seeds now compile with AllInstances alone; the smoke test requires that stronger result without changing their language fixtures or expected output.
- **Remaining scope:** H09b5c2 retains method/receiver and wrapper/adapter precision, broader return/capture/container/caller flow, and C251 containing-code caps, split further by D-202 below. H09b5b's refused independent acceptance remains blocked; H09b5d's same-IR identity and dynamic variant cap are unchanged. See [c1 evidence](agent/hardening-evidence/h09b5c1.md) for actual acceptance status and measurements.

### 9.139 Returned ordinary function-value sources (H09b5c2a)

- **Lowering** (D-202): `valueSources` follows a call's proven callee set through `callSources` (`returned_values.go`), joining each callee's declaration-shared `returnSources`. Only synchronous non-generic ordinary declarations, expressions and arrows qualify; the checker's signature, not just syntax, determines genericity (including contextual generic factories). Expression-bodied arrows use their expression; `bodyReturnSources` traverses all explicit returns, including loops, switches, catch/finally and unreachable arms, skipping nested callable/class bodies. Bare returns and fallthrough add no function source. Unknown arms, mutation, uncertain callees, recursive cycles, async/generator/generic factories or exhaustion keep compatible instances. The existing depth/work/source-count limits apply to body visits and callee-summary joins too. A returned parameter uses D-201's all-caller proof, not a call-specific argument environment; source AST identities still stand for all enclosing instances and captures.
- **Tests:** `lower/returned_value_sources_test.go` checks exact/unknown return sets, nested-body exclusion, shared parameters, body-work and depth limits, summary reuse and contextual-generic exclusion. `lower/returned_value_bounds_test.go` checks charged cached-callee joins, the exact 256/257 source-union boundary, cached unknowns and extra async/generator/mutation/cycle exclusions. `driver/returned_value_sources_test.go` checks required and excluded pairs directly with AllInstances, unknown-return retention (also contextual generic factories) and shared-caller unions. `run/generic_value_flow_returned_{sources,unknowns,enclosing_captures}.pts` check captures across generic function/class instances, aliases/chains, merged/finally returns, undefined arms, mutation, any/unknown, callbacks and cycles against expected output and Node.
- **Boundary:** no method/receiver/wrapper proof, narrowing/fallback ordering, runtime conversion or checker-identity change. H09b5c2b retains broader flow and C251; H09b5b's refused review is not replaced. Actual execution, measurements and acceptance status are recorded in [c2a evidence](agent/hardening-evidence/h09b5c2a.md).

### 9.140 Exact wrapper and captured-value sources (H09b5c2b2a)

- **Lowering** (D-205): `boundMethod` associates an ordinary forwarder with an exact creation site. `wrapperSource` accepts property-read sites and normalizes default-library `.bind` callees to their complete creation call; other/synthetic reads stay unknown. A bounded lexical walk rejects generic classes and generic function signatures, including contextual generic arrows/expressions. `addForwarder` registers accepted sites only where forwarders are actually recorded. Source queries reuse the existing depth-128, work-4,096 and union-256 limits; registered sites are singleton opaque tokens, not receiver-class proofs. A cached unknown can remain unknown after late registration (lost precision, not omitted instances). Named method demands and forwarders without known origin retain compatible pairings.
- **Captured calls:** `valueForward` carries the captured bind target or method-field initializer/shorthand expression into the synthetic demand. A non-generic instantiating adapter carries its adapted expression (the operand for an explicit instantiation). Ordinary source/alias/closed-parameter/return proofs apply to those inner value demands. The outer caller's source is never substituted for the captured expression. Closure descriptors still share the actual wrapper's captures; method dispatch, fallback/trial rollback and checked-any conversions are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `driver/wrapper_value_sources_test.go` asserts required and excluded direct-AllInstances target pairs for method reads, aliases, closed parameters, merges/returns, bind chains, non-generic contexts and method-field forwarders; unknown/any/mutable origins retain compatible targets. `lower/wrapper_creation_tables_test.go` checks wrapper descriptor signatures/capture sharing, required/excluded receiver targets (refined by D-207 below) and shared-generic wrappers. `lower/wrapper_sources_test.go` pins syntactic/checker-contextual generic ancestors, depth/parens/work exhaustion and cached unknown registration. Run/Node fixtures `generic_wrapper_flow_{proven_sources,unknown_origins,receivers_and_captures}.pts` cover repeated receivers/captures, overrides and explicit thisArg. C254's pre-existing inline asserted-any adaptation gap is separately skipped in `generic_asserted_any_nongeneric_adapter.pts`; C255's contextual-generic arrow returning a bound closure is skipped in `generic_contextual_returned_bind.pts`. Exact-base/current direct-native comparisons establish both as existing H29 issues, not accepted optimization regressions. The unchanged unrelated-adapter fallback seed now must compile with AllInstances alone; its obsolete smoke exemption is removed.
- **Boundary:** broader receiver dispatch and source flow remain H09b5c2b2b; no parent completion or H09b5b acceptance. Compatible-candidate enumeration is not claimed eliminated. Actual local gates, retained m4 method measurements and scoped independent review are in [c2b2a evidence](agent/hardening-evidence/h09b5c2b2a.md).

### 9.141 Captured method-wrapper receiver sources (H09b5c2b2b2a)

- **Lowering** (D-207): `methodForward` carries the actual captured receiver expression in its synthetic demand's `receiverSource`, using D-206's separate bounded receiver proof (`receiver_sources.go`). Ordinary class/interface property reads supply their object's expression; `methodCallOrBind` supplies the actual `thisArg`, never the object used to look up the method. `genericMethodOf`/`objectMethodForwardFrom` preserve that distinction. Synthetic/destructured reads supply nil; the bounded lexical walk rejects enclosing generic classes/functions, including checker-contextually generic functions.
- **Conservatism:** receiver sources follow immutable aliases, closed named non-generic parameter caller unions and ordinary return summaries within the existing depth-128, work-4,096 and union-256 limits. Only actual directly named, unwritten, non-generic constructions with no constructor expression-return in any ordinary named ancestor prove a class. Dynamic/any/unknown storage, mutable receivers, generic/uncertain constructors and ancestors, replacement returns, cycles and exhausted proofs retain compatible methods. A shared source AST represents every receiver it can capture, not the outer consumer's particular call. Every forwarding signature still shares the original wrapper's actual capture locals; the proof never substitutes a class for a runtime receiver. Inherited implementations and override barriers remain available.
- **Tests:** `driver/h09_captured_receiver_flow_test.go` checks required/excluded AllInstances method/class-descriptor pairs and forwarding signatures for direct/aliased/closed-parameter/returned/merged receivers, bind chains and non-generic adapters, plus actual bind-thisArg, shared-site joins, uncertain/generic/pattern origins, inheritance, literal candidates and depth/work exhaustion. `lower/captured_receiver_tables_test.go` checks actual capture sharing, all-caller joins, unknown/pattern retention and the 256/257-source boundary. `lower/captured_receiver_write_sources_test.go` checks that assignment targets wrapped in `as`, type assertions, non-null or satisfies (also inside patterns/closures) enter the shared mutation index and leave receiver/function parameter sources unknown (C258). These tsc-valid assignments remain unsupported by native lowering; the guards protect the proof, not newly claimed syntax support. The old wrapper-creation characterization now requires diagonal targets for its exact closed callers rather than obsolete cross-pairs; its signature/capture assertions are unchanged. Run/Node fixtures `generic_captured_receiver_{sources,unknowns}.pts` cover repeated captures, independent state, inherited/overridden methods, explicit thisArg, aliases/returns, any/unknown/mutation/property/escaped/generic/cyclic sources.
- **Boundary:** no runtime, checked-any, checker identity, fallback ordering or static-type compatibility-cache change. Broader capture/container/caller/return flow remains H09b5c2b2b2b, all parents stay open and H09b5b's refused independent review is untouched. Actual RED/GREEN, scoped independent review, deterministic growth and local acceptance status are in [b2a evidence](agent/hardening-evidence/h09b5c2b2b2a.md).

### 9.142 Instances for type arguments of one IR type (H09b5d)

- **Lowering** (D-216): `genericInstanceFor` (`generics.go`) keys a function's instances by their type arguments' IR types and literal types (`instance.literals`), and where the declaration computes types from its type parameters (`computedTypes`, cached in `lowerer.computingDecls`: the types in its signature, its body's expressions and type nodes and its calls' type arguments that `computesTypes` finds, mentioning them, at most `maxComputedTypes`), also by `typeArgumentsIdentity` (`instance.identity`): those types' keyParts under the candidate's substitution (`instanceSubstitution`), in `keyIdentity`'s brands, `typeArguments` (object types and program interfaces by their members, `isProgramInterface`, no IR type made, one part past the bounds) and `widenLiterals` modes. `madeFor` and `maxDynamicVariants` are gone. Type arguments keep their brands (`typeArgument`, which shares `substituted` and `normalized` with `instantiate`). `demandAt` and `instantiatingAdapterSkipping` (`forwarding.go`) give every typed demand its `instanceKey` (with brands; the function type's declaration only for a dynamic result). `lower.ProgramSplittingClasses` sets `lowerer.splitClasses`: `classInstanceOf` (`generic_classes.go`) then reuses an instance of the same IR types only where `sharesClass` holds (`classMembersIdentity`, cached in `lowerer.classIdentities`, over `classComputedTypes`' computed superclass and members, written by `memberPart`; a differing member converts one way and names the same classes, `classesIn`), and `computesTypes` counts a reference to a class with computed types as computing. The driver's `compileC` (`pts/driver/driver.go`) lowers with classes shared and, only if no lowering of the fallback chain succeeds, again splitting them (`checkedProgram.splitClasses`), keeping the first failure. `narrowedInstance` gives a dynamic-IR type argument narrowed to no value unknown's narrowing; `storedDynamic` (`dynamic.go`) takes an instance's variable whose instantiated declared type has a dynamic IR type as stored dynamic.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/generic_instances_same_ir_type_{promises,twins}.pts`, `generic_class_instances_literal_type_arguments.pts`, `generic_instances_branded_type_arguments.pts`, `generic_values_same_ir_type_promises.pts`, `generic_dynamic_variants_past_cap.pts` (unskipped); `generic_narrowed_instances_{wider_first,dynamic_arguments}.pts`; the reproducer's 63 `generic_*` tests (body-only through every call form, classes, brands, dynamic results, recursion; C260–C262 skipped); 42 from review rounds 1 and 2's probes (class conversions the base ran, indexed members, phantoms, recursion through promises, computed superclasses, shared-class factories and inheritance, keyed call forms, interface results; C263, C264, C267, C268 skipped); `generic_instanceof_map_unknown.pts` and `generic_instanceof_map_promise.pts` (C265); `lower_test.go`'s `TestInstancesForTypeArgumentsOfOneIRTypeAreToldApartWhereTheirTypesDiffer` and `TestClassInstancesOfOneIRTypeAreSplitOnlyWhereTheirComputedTypesCantShare`; IR goldens print typed instance calls' keys.
- **Boundary:** classes keep D-050's nominal instances, split only where a program doesn't lower otherwise (C260–C263); typed demands' entries per key and the linear lookup are C266, deferred to H27. Evidence: [H09b5d](agent/hardening-evidence/h09b5d.md).

### 9.143 References narrowed by `instanceof` to a generic class (H10a)

- **Lowering** (D-218): `instanceofNarrowing` (`pts/lower/instanceof.go`) recognizes a reference (a variable, parameter, property or literal-key element) whose type at the node is a generic class's prototype type, `C<any, …>` (`isPrototypeInstance`), or a type parameter's intersection with it, where its declared type (`declaredTypeOfReference`: also private names, index signatures and imported bindings) is neither that nor a union holding it; the checker's `PtsInferInstanceTypeArguments` gives C's instance where the declared type implies one alone: through `ptsHeritageTypes`, the type arguments C's `extends` chain passes up as they are for a class source, and structural inference, agreeing with what `implements` passes up, for an interface or object type; per member of a union, which must agree and none of which may hold any instance. Results are cached per reference, tsc's type and declared type. `typeAt` and `receiverClass` give the inferred instance for the reference, `narrowed` converts the reference to it with D-149's checked downcast, and `classCast` converts it to another instance of C (where tsc's C<any, …> goes) with a check where it runs. A member read or method call on it whose type in tsc mentions `any` (`isInstanceofDynamicMember`, `mentionsAny`) is boxed (`instanceofMember`) and stays the instance's own value as a dynamic value (`isNarrowedDynamic`, `storedDynamic`, `isMemberOfNarrowedInstance`); calls of function-valued fields and of methods (not private ones) with literal or callback arguments for such parameters are dynamic method calls (`callsInstanceofMethodDynamically`). Where such a member goes into a place a dynamic value would be copied into (`copiesDynamicValue`), it stays typed, so D-094's copy rules apply (PTS1001 where a copy would be changed or kept); an un-annotated variable or `for…of` binding initialized from such a dynamic value (`holdsInstanceofDynamicValue`) holds it as a dynamic value (`isInstanceofDynamicLocal`, `bindingType`), with no copy; members tsc types C<any, …> stay dynamic values in `narrowedMember`, `familyPrivateField` and `optionalChainFrom`. `copiesInstanceofDynamicValue` (in `convert` and `nullishCoalescing`) reports D-094's PTS1001 where such a dynamic value would be converted into a type that holds dynamic values, except for a parameter only read (`isReadOnlyArgument`) and a read-only `.par` view (`isReadOnlyParallelView`); `bindingSource` gives pattern-bound names their source (`callbackSource` a callback parameter's: the receiver of the method call it is an argument of), and `isInstanceofDynamicLocal` caches per symbol and substitution; the guard applies to values H10a made dynamic (`madeDynamicByInstanceof`), not to members of `unknown`, `any` or `catch` bindings, which copy as D-179 says; `parameterType` makes callback parameters of such methods dynamic values, un-annotated ones and annotated ones of a copying type that the method may hand the instance's objects (`handsInstanceObjects`, `arrayCallbackObjectParameters`), `resultType` a callback's undeclared copying result (`returnsToDynamicInstanceCall`), and `dynamicCallWith` reports, through `copiesCallbackArgument`, functions given by reference or in literal arguments with such parameters (unless `callbackFunction`'s known code `onlyReadsParameter`); objects inside such values (`isInstanceofNestedObject`) stay dynamic values in `isNarrowedDynamic`, `narrowedValue`, `narrowedMember`, `optionalChainFrom` and `as`, and `arrayValues` spreads them into `concat` (session 6); `narrowingAnswers` keeps `isNarrowedDynamic`'s and `instanceofDynamicSource`'s answers within one question, which a member chain asks of each object twice; read-only and new-array copies are allowed only where the copy's elements aren't copies (`copiesElements`, `copiesInstanceElements` in `convert`'s D-094 case), a by-reference function's array parameter it hands a callback isn't only read (`handsToCallback`), spread arguments are checked (`copiesSpreadCallbacks`), and `.par` views of nested arrays and new arrays use the instance's types (`instanceTypeOf`, `instanceResultType`); reduce's accumulator is handed objects unless the callback owns it (`ownsAccumulator`), array members' `push`/`unshift`/`splice`/`fill` keep their arguments (`keepingArrayMethods`), and `assignment`, `destructuringAssignment` and `Object.assign` report functions with copying parameters stored into such values (`storesCopyingFunction`, `isInstanceofDynamicPlace`) unless they are the instance's own; untyped locals bound to the instance's functions hold them as they are, called (and `call`ed, `apply`d, `bind`ed) as through `any` (`call`, `functionMethodCall`); `awaitPoint` keeps an awaited such value dynamic. Where nothing is inferred (`isUninferredInstanceof`), `narrowed` boxes the reference, except as the object of a private member written or called (`isPrivateMemberChange`), and `isNarrowedDynamic` makes its members dynamic reads, writes and calls; `familyPrivateField` reads a private field `o.#f` of such an instance (in `propertyAccess` and `dynamicRead`) through `ir.FamilyField`, which cgen's `familyField` writes as tests of each of C's instances in turn (the list is complete when it is written) and V8's TypeError for another object. `ensureMembers` restores the parameter locals of the class's members after declaring an instance's members (`memberParameterLocals`), since an instance made inside a method of its own class declares its own for the same symbols.
- **Tests:** `pts/tests/run/generic_instanceof_*.pts` (the reproducer's 12 RED tests and 4 guards; the parent's `par_callbacks` at threads 1 2 4, `union_conflicting_instances`, `generic_function_instances`; 27 from review round 1's probes; 17 from review round 2's probes and the member chains of round 1's, `dynamic_member_locals`; `errors/generic_instanceof_{unrelated_class_target,member_passed_to_mutator,member_returned}.pts` (honest PTS1001); 9 from review round 3's probes and `errors/generic_instanceof_{dynamic_value_sinks,uninferred_value_sinks,dynamic_value_yield_sinks}.pts`; `flows_out`, `unknown_flows_out` and `par` skipped for H10b); `lower_test.go`'s `TestInstanceofNarrowsToTheInstanceTheDeclaredTypeImplies`; the corpus's `algorithms/lru_cache_map.pts`, unskipped.
- **Boundary:** a narrowed value, or a member tsc types C<any, …>, that flows into a place tsc types `C<any, …>` (`const y = x`, `let n = c.next`, `[x]`, `ident(x)`, an inferred return type, `find`/`filter` with an inferred predicate) compiles and throws a checked TypeError where it runs (H10b, C279); private writes and private method calls on an instance held as a dynamic value that isn't an instance for any throw TypeError (C274's rest, H10b). Evidence: [H10a](agent/hardening-evidence/h10a.md).
